Experiments on mathematical reasoning and tool-use benchmarks across multiple model scales demonstrate that PBSD consistently achieves the strongest average performance among comparable baselines, showing improved training stability over prior self-distillation baselines while preserving token efficiency.

</details>


### [152] [HEXST: Hexagonal Shifted-Window Transformer for Spatial Transcriptomics Gene Expression Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04682)
*Keunho Byeon,Jin Tae Kwak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HEXST是一种几何对齐的Transformer模型，通过处理六边形点坐标和对比敏感差分目标，从组织学图像中预测空间基因表达，在七个数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学成本高、通量有限，难以大规模部署。现有计算方法大多忽略六边形采样结构，且点对点回归目标导致基因表达过度平滑，掩盖了基因特异性空间异质性。

Method: HEXST直接在六边形点坐标上操作，通过定制的移位窗口注意力机制和六边形旋转位置编码实现高效局部到全局上下文建模，并引入对比敏感差分目标和单细胞基础模型转录组先验。

Result: 在七个空间转录组数据集上，HEXST consistently outperforms现有最先进模型，提供准确且稳健的空间基因表达预测，同时保持基因对比度和空间异质性。

Conclusion: HEXST通过几何对齐的Transformer设计有效解决了六边形采样结构适配和基因表达过度平滑问题，为空间转录组学的常规化应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics offers spatially resolved gene expression profiling within tissue sections, but its cost and limited throughput hinder large-scale deployment. To extend this capability to routine practice, recent computational methods aim to infer spatial gene expression directly from ubiquitous hematoxylin and eosin-stained histology slides. However, most existing models assume Cartesian or geometry-agnostic locality, despite the hexagonal sampling of widely used spot-array platforms, and point-wise regression objectives often yield over-smoothed gene expression profiles, obscuring gene-specific spatial heterogeneity. To address these, we propose HEXST, a geometry-aligned Transformer for spatial gene expression prediction from histology. HEXST operates directly on hexagonal spot coordinates to enable efficient local-to-global contextual modeling via tailored shifted-window attention mechanism and hexagonal rotary positional encoding. To enhance gene-wise spatial contrast, HEXST complements point-wise regression with a contrast-sensitive differential objective and transcriptomic priors from a pretrained single-cell foundation model during training. Across seven spatial transcriptomics datasets, HEXST consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, providing accurate and robust spatial gene expression predictions while preserving gene-wise contrast and spatial heterogeneity.

</details>


### [153] [Continual Knowledge Updating in LLM Systems: Learning Through Multi-Timescale Memory Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05097)
*Andreas Pattichis,Constantine Dovrolis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Memini构建了一种受生物记忆启发的关联记忆系统，通过快慢耦合的双变量机制实现外部记忆的自主重组，使新信息快速可用、重复信息巩固、无关信息自然遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM部署后外部记忆需要显式管理，无法像生物记忆那样自主适应环境变化。

Method: 采用Benna-Fusi突触巩固模型，在有向图中为每条边维护快慢两个耦合变量，模拟生物记忆的多时间尺度动力学。

Result: 该系统自然涌现出 episodic sensitivity（情节敏感性）、gradual consolidation（渐进巩固）和selective forgetting（选择性遗忘）三大特性。

Conclusion: 应将外部记忆视为具有自身学习动力学的可重组基质，而非静态存储。

Abstract: LLMs are trained once, then deployed into a world that never stops changing. External memory compensates for this, but most systems manage it explicitly rather than letting it adapt on its own. Biological memory works differently: coupled multi-timescale dynamics make new associations immediately usable, strengthen what repetition confirms, and let the rest fade. We argue that external memory should follow a similar principle. In Memini, this view takes the form of an associative memory that organizes knowledge as a directed graph. Each edge carries two coupled internal variables, one fast and one slow, following the Benna-Fusi model of synaptic consolidation. From this coupling, episodic sensitivity, gradual consolidation, and selective forgetting emerge as facets of a single mechanism, reframing external memory as a learning substrate that reorganizes through its own dynamics.

</details>


### [154] [Uncertainty-Aware Exploratory Direct Preference Optimization for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04874)
*Huatian Zhang,Zhendong Mao,Lei Zhang,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UE-DPO方法，通过token级认知不确定性引导MLLMs主动探索并自我纠正视觉幻觉，解决了DPO中自参考偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPO方法依赖模型自评估的视觉敏感性分配训练权重，但受训练中模型自身的自参考偏差影响，会强化已学好的视觉线索而忽略难以感知的关键细节，限制了深度对齐。

Method: 提出UE-DPO方法：1) 量化模型在图像中无法正确预测token的不确定性；2) 基于不确定性感知的探索强度，在偏好样本中对视觉缺陷token施加更多学习压力，同时在非偏好样本中减轻对有益知识的过度惩罚。

Result: 大量实验证明了UE-DPO方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: UE-DPO通过认知不确定性引导，使模型能够主动发现自身认知缺陷并自我纠正，实现了更优的多模态大模型视觉幻觉缓解。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has proven to be an effective solution for mitigating hallucination in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) by learning from preference pairs. One of its key challenges lies in how to transfer the sequence-level preference into fine-grained supervision on visual fidelity. To safeguard vision-related tokens that are prone to hallucination, existing methods typically allocate training emphasis according to the model's self-assessed visual sensitivity signals. However, such sensitivity, estimated by a model still under training, introduces self-referential bias: reinforcing already well-learned visual cues while neglecting hard-to-perceive but critical details, thereby limiting deeper alignment. In this work, we propose an Uncertainty-aware Exploratory Direct Preference Optimization (UE-DPO) method for MLLMs, which enables the model to uncover its cognitive deficiencies and actively explore for self-correction, guided by token-level epistemic uncertainty. Specifically, we first quantify the uncertainty from the model's failure to ground token predictions in the given image. Then, based on an uncertainty-aware exploration intensity, we encourage more learning pressure on visually deficient tokens in preferred samples, and alleviate the over-penalization of beneficial knowledge in dispreferred samples. Further, we provide a theoretical justification for our method, and extensive experiments demonstrate its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>


### [155] [On the Wasserstein Gradient Flow Interpretation of Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05118)
*Arthur Gretton,Li Kevin Wenliang,Alexandre Galashov,James Thornton,Valentin De Bortoli,Arnaud Doucet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从Wasserstein梯度流视角分析Generative Modeling via Drifting (GMD)框架，发现其理论算法与实现算法存在差异，并将GMD思想推广到MMD、 sliced Wasserstein距离等更多散度上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从Wasserstein梯度流（WGF）的理论框架出发，重新审视和解释近期提出的GMD生成式建模方法，揭示其理论基础与算法实现之间的差异。

Method: 将GMD视为WGF的不动点求解问题，分别分析了GMD的理论算法（对应KL散度上的WGF，带Parzen平滑）和实际实现算法（对应Sinkhorn散度上的WGF不动点，但缺乏某些优良性质），并将该思想推广到MMD、sliced Wasserstein距离、GAN判别函数等其他WGF。

Result: 证明了GMD的理论算法与实现算法存在本质差异；展示了GMD思想可推广至多种WGF（MMD、sliced Wasserstein距离、GAN等）。

Conclusion: GMD实际上是对WGF框架的一种新的近似求解视角，但理论算法与实际实现之间存在不一致性；该视角可扩展至更广泛的生成式散度中。

Abstract: Recently, Deng et al. (2026) proposed Generative Modeling via Drifting (GMD), a novel framework for generative tasks. This note presents an analysis of GMD through the lens of Wasserstein Gradient Flows (WGF), i.e., the path of steepest descent for a functional in the space of probability measures, equipped with the geometry of optimal transport. Unlike previous WGF-based contributions, GMD can be thought of as directly targeting a fixed point of a specific WGF flow. We demonstrate three main results: first, that one algorithm proposed by Deng et al. (2026) corresponds to finding the limiting point of a WGF on the KL divergence, with Parzen smoothing on the densities. Second, that the algorithm actually implemented by Deng et al. (2026) corresponds to a different procedure, which bears some resemblance to the fixed point of a WGF on the Sinkhorn divergence, but lacks certain desirable properties of the latter. Third, the same same idea can be extended to the limiting point of other WGFs, including the Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), the sliced Wasserstein distance, and GAN critic functions.

</details>


### [156] [Adaptive Policy Selection and Fine-Tuning under Interaction Budgets for Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05123)
*Alper Kamil Bozkurt,Xiaoan Xu,Shangtong Zhang,Miroslav Pajic,Yuichi Motai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应的策略选择与微调方法，用于解决离线到在线强化学习（O2O-RL）中策略部署后是否可微调的不确定性问题，在有限在线交互预算下，通过上置信界（UCB）方法动态选择策略进行微调，在多个基准上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有O2O-RL流程存在两个主要问题：1）离策略评估（OPE）不可靠，仅凭其选择策略有风险，而在线评估（OE）又浪费本可用于微调的交互资源；2）无法预先确定预训练策略在部署后是否能通过微调提升，尤其是在非平稳环境中，导致静态部署单一策略的策略在实践中不可行。

Method: 首先使用不同的离线RL算法和超参数训练一组候选策略，通过OPE获得初始性能估计；然后基于上置信界（UCB）方法自适应地选择策略进行微调，根据预测性能动态分配在线交互预算，以高效利用有限的在线交互资源。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，所提出的自适应方法优于现有的O2O-RL基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过自适应策略选择与微调，可以在有限在线交互预算下有效解决O2O-RL中策略部署与微调的不确定性问题，提升整体性能。

Abstract: In offline-to-online reinforcement learning (O2O-RL), policies are first safely trained offline using previously collected datasets and then further fine-tuned for tasks via limited online interactions. In a typical O2O-RL pipeline, candidate policies trained with offline RL are evaluated via either off-policy evaluation (OPE) or online evaluation (OE). The policy with the highest estimated value is then deployed and continually fine-tuned. However, this setup has two main issues. First, OPE can be unreliable, making it risky to deploy a policy based solely on those estimates, whereas OE may identify a viable policy with substantial online interaction, which could have been used for fine-tuning. Second--and more importantly--it is also often not possible to determine a priori whether a pretrained policy will improve with post-deployment fine-tuning, especially in non-stationary environments. As a result, procedures committing to a single deployed policy are impractical in many real-world settings. Moreover, a naive remedy that exhaustively fine-tunes all candidates would violate interaction budget constraints and is likewise infeasible. In this paper, we propose a novel adaptive approach for policy selection and fine-tuning under online interaction budgets in O2O-RL. Following the standard pipeline, we first train a set of candidate policies with different offline RL algorithms and hyperparameters; we then perform OPE to obtain initial performance estimates. We next adaptively select and fine-tune the policies based on their predicted performance via an upper-confidence-bound approach thereby making efficient use of online interactions. We demonstrate that our approach improves upon O2O-RL baselines with various benchmarks.

</details>


### [157] [Joint Treatment Effect Estimation from Incomplete Healthcare Data: Temporal Causal Normalizing Flows with LLM-driven Evolutionary MNAR Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05125)
*Olivia Jullian Parra,Sara Zoccheddu,David Catalan Cerezo,Tom Forzy,Franziska Ulrich,William Sutcliffe,Jakob Martin Burgstaller,Oliver Senn,Patrick Owen,Nicola Serra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段流水线，先通过DAG约束的归一化流（CausalFlow-T）进行精确反事实推断，再结合LLM驱动的进化插补器处理MNAR缺失数据，用于从电子健康记录中估计因果治疗效果。在合成和半合成基准上验证了方法的有效性，并在瑞士2型糖尿病真实数据上得到与随机对照试验一致的结果（GLP-1受体激动剂相比SGLT-2抑制剂减重多0.98 kg）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机对照试验有时不可行，而使用电子健康记录等观察性数据进行因果推断时，存在时间依赖性混杂和高达50%-80%的非随机缺失数据问题，现有方法通常将因果估计、缺失值处理和时序结构分开处理，缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 提出两阶段流水线：(1) CausalFlow-T，一种DAG约束的归一化流，结合LSTM编码患者历史，实现精确可逆的反事实推断，避免变分推断的近似误差；(2) LLM驱动的进化插补器，通过进化搜索提出可执行的插补算子（而非单个数值），利用大语言模型处理MNAR缺失。

Result: 在四个合成和一个半合成基准上验证了DAG约束和精确推断各自解决不同失败模式；在30%-80% MNAR缺失率下，LLM进化插补器在生物标志物和因果指标上整体排名最优；在瑞士2型糖尿病真实EHR数据上，估计的减重差异为-0.98 kg [95% CI -1.01, -0.96]，与随机对照试验证据一致。

Conclusion: 该两阶段流水线能够在缺失严重的真实世界纵向EHR中可靠地估计治疗效果，得到的因果结论与随机对照试验结果一致，为解决观察性数据中的因果推断问题提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Target trial emulation (TTE) enables causal questions to be studied with observational data when randomized controlled trials (RCTs) are infeasible. Yet treatment-effect methods often address causal estimation, missingness, and temporal structure separately, limiting their robustness in electronic health records (EHRs), where time-varying confounding and missing-not-at-random (MNAR) biomarkers can reach 50%--80%. We propose a two-stage pipeline for treatment effect estimation from incomplete longitudinal EHRs. First, CausalFlow-T, a directed acyclic graph (DAG)-constrained normalizing flow with long short-term memory (LSTM)-encoded patient history, performs exact invertible counterfactual inference, avoiding approximation errors from variational inference and separating confounding through explicit causal structure. Ablations on four synthetic and one semi-synthetic benchmark with known counterfactuals show that DAG constraints and exact inference address distinct failure modes: neither compensates for the other. Second, because CausalFlow-T requires completed inputs, we introduce an LLM-driven evolutionary imputer that proposes executable imputation operators rather than individual entries, and evaluate it with three large language model (LLM) backends, including two open-source models. Across 30%--80% MNAR missingness, this imputer achieves the best pooled rank over biomarker and causal metrics, leading in point-wise accuracy and temporal extrapolation while preserving average treatment effect (ATE) recovery as statistical baselines degrade. On Swiss primary-care EHRs from adults with type 2 diabetes initiating a GLP-1 receptor agonist or SGLT-2 inhibitor, the pipeline estimates a per-protocol weight-loss difference of -0.98 kg [95% CI -1.01, -0.96] favoring GLP-1 receptor agonists, consistent with randomized evidence and obtained from realistically incomplete real-world EHRs.

</details>


### [158] [Ensuring Reliability in Programming Knowledge Tracing: A Re-evaluation of Attention-augmented Models and Experimental Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04727)
*Jaewook Kim,Hyeoncheol Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了编程知识追踪（PKT）模型，发现注意力维度设置、序列构建方式等实验细节会显著影响性能评估，在严格控制条件下，注意力增强模型与标准DKT的性能差距大幅缩小，复杂架构并不总是带来更好表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PKT模型虽然报告了强劲的实验性能，但其可靠性容易受到微妙的实现和实验设计选择的影响，需要重新审视和规范评估方法。

Method: 通过网格搜索选择超参数，固定交叉验证中所有折的超参数设置；纠正注意力维度设置问题；确保按ServerTimestamp正确排序学生作答序列；系统探索最大序列长度的影响。

Result: 在受控且一致的设置下，注意力增强模型与标准DKT的性能差距显著减小；增加的架构复杂性并不一致地转化为更优的性能。

Conclusion: 本研究为编程知识追踪领域提供了可靠、可比较评估的实践指导，强调了实验设计规范化的重要性。

Abstract: Programming Knowledge Tracing (PKT) has recently advanced through hybrid approaches that integrate attention-based feature modeling for code representation with RNN-based sequential prediction. While these models report strong empirical performance, their reliability can be sensitive to subtle implementation and experimental design choices. This study revisits representative PKT models and shows that reported gains can be substantially influenced by model configuration and sequence construction practices. We identify issues in attention dimension settings that affect performance estimates, and demonstrate that improper ordering of student attempts, such as ignoring ServerTimestamp, can violate temporal causality and lead to overly optimistic results. To ensure consistent evaluation, hyperparameters are selected via grid search guided by a single designated fold and then fixed uniformly across all folds during cross-validation. We further analyze the role of assignment-wise characteristics and systematically explore the impact of maximum sequence length. Using this protocol, we re-evaluate PKT models on the CodeWorkout dataset. Our results show that, under controlled and consistent settings, the performance gap between attention-enhanced models and standard DKT is significantly reduced, and increased architectural complexity does not consistently translate into superior performance. Beyond individual model comparisons, this work provides practical guidance for reliable and comparable evaluation in programming knowledge tracing.

</details>


### [159] [Using Common Random Numbers for Simulation-based Planning with Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04732)
*Sandarbh Yadav,Frederic J Maliakkal,Harshad Khadilkar,Shivaram Kalyanakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在基于仿真的规划中使用公共随机数（Common Random Numbers）来降低相对效用方差的方法，并通过实验验证了该方案能提升任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机环境下的仿真规划中，重复调用采样模型来估计各动作的效用时，估计方差会影响决策质量。本文旨在通过引入公共随机数来降低相对效用的方差，从而提升规划性能。

Method: 提出了一种简单的方法：当仿真调用超过一定深度的rollout策略时，使用公共随机数生成轨迹，从而（可证明地）降低相对效用的方差。

Result: 合成任务实验证实了该方案能提升任务性能；在养老金发放任务中的单步前瞻规划以及Ludo游戏中的UCT算法部署这两个实际应用场景中，也体现了该创新的广泛意义。

Conclusion: 公共随机数技术可有效降低仿真规划中的方差，提升决策质量，在多个实际任务中具有显著应用价值。

Abstract: Simulation-based planning with rollouts is a widely-deployed technique for decision making in stochastic environments. The primary instrument of simulation-based planning is a sampling model, which is repeatedly called to generate trajectories and estimate the utilities of available actions. Among the actions thus explored, one with the maximum estimated utility is then executed. In this paper, we examine the effect of using common random numbers in the simulation process. We obtain a simple recipe for (provably) reducing variance in relative utility when simulations invoke a rollout policy beyond some depth. Experiments on synthetic tasks confirm that our scheme improves task performance. The broader significance of our innovation is apparent from two practical applications: (1) single-step lookahead planning in a pension-disbursement task, and (2) a deployment of the well-known UCT algorithm for the game of Ludo.

</details>


### [160] [Superposition Is Not Necessary: A Mechanistic Interpretability Analysis of Transformer Representations for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05151)
*Alper Yıldırım*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过稀疏自编码器（SAEs）对PatchTST的内部表示进行机制可解释性分析，发现在时间序列预测中，Transformer的FFN层并未依赖叠加（superposition）机制，表示始终保持稀疏且对干预不敏感，这解释了为何简单线性模型（如DLinear）在该任务上仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer在时间序列预测中被广泛采用，但其在NLP中成功的表示机制（如叠加）是否真正在时间序列数据上发挥作用尚不清楚，且简单线性模型的持续竞争力缺乏机制性解释。

Method: 1) 首先证明单层窄维度Transformer在基准测试中能达到与深层配置相当的预测性能；2) 训练稀疏自编码器（SAEs）分析PatchTST中FFN层的中间激活，字典大小为原生维度的0.5x至4.0x；3) 评估字典扩展对性能的影响及潜在特征的因果干预效果。

Result: 1) 扩展字典仅带来平均0.214%的性能变化，过完备字典中大部分神经元保持非活跃；2) 对主导潜在特征进行因果干预时，预测扰动极小；3) 在所有设置中均未发现FFN表示依赖强叠加的证据，表示保持稀疏、稳定且对干预不敏感。

Conclusion: 叠加对于时间序列预测中的竞争性能并非必要，标准预测基准可能不需要驱动Transformer在语言建模中成功的丰富组合表示，这有助于理解简单线性模型为何在该领域持续具有竞争力。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have been widely adopted for time series forecasting, yet whether the representational mechanisms that make them powerful in NLP actually engage on time series data remains unexplored. The persistent competitiveness of simple linear models such as DLinear has fueled ongoing debate, but no mechanistic explanation for this phenomenon has been offered. We address this gap by applying sparse autoencoders (SAEs), a tool from mechanistic interpretability, to probe the internal representations of PatchTST. We first establish that a single-layer, narrow-dimensional transformer matches the forecasting performance of deeper configurations across commonly used benchmarks. We then train SAEs on the post-GELU intermediate FFN activations with dictionary sizes ranging from 0.5x to 4.0x the native dimensionality. Expanding the dictionary yields negligible downstream performance change (average 0.214%), with large portions of overcomplete dictionaries remaining inactive. Targeted causal interventions on dominant latent features produce minimal forecast perturbation. Across all evaluated settings, we observe no empirical evidence that the analyzed FFN representations rely on strong superposition. Instead, the representations remain sparse, stable under aggressive dictionary expansion, and largely insensitive to latent interventions. These results demonstrate that superposition is not necessary for competitive performance on standard forecasting benchmarks, suggesting they may not demand the rich compositional representations that drive transformer success in language modeling, and helping explain the persistent competitiveness of simple linear models

</details>


### [161] [OSAQ: Outlier Self-Absorption for Accurate Low-bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04738)
*Zhikai Li,Zhen Dong,Xuewen Liu,Jing Zhang,Qingyi Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OSAQ方法，利用Hessian矩阵的低秩特性找到零空间，通过加性权重变换抑制离群值，实现低比特量化而无需推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM参数量大导致推理资源消耗高，后训练权重量化虽能缓解，但权重中的系统性离群值严重影响低比特量化性能，现有方法效果仍不理想。

Method: 观察到Hessian矩阵在不同输入下具有低秩一致性，存在稳定零空间；在该零空间中构造加性权重变换，通过闭式解高效实现，离群值被抑制而不影响任务损失，且可离线吸收到权重中。

Result: 2比特量化下，OSAQ结合GPTQ相比原始GPTQ困惑度降低超过40%。

Conclusion: OSAQ有效抑制离群值，显著提升低比特量化性能，无推理开销，无需层间变换或迭代训练。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities. However, their massive parameter scale leads to significant resource consumption and latency during inference. Post-training weight-only quantization offers a promising solution by reducing model size and accelerating token generation through alleviating the memory-bound issue. Nevertheless, the presence of inherent systematic outliers in weights continues to be a major obstacle. While existing methods, such as scaling and rotation, attempt to address this issue, the performance remains unsatisfactory. In this paper, we propose Outlier Self-Absorption Quantization (OSAQ), which performs additive weight suppression guided by the second-order low-rank property for low-bit weight-only quantization of LLMs. Specifically, we observe that the Hessian exhibits low-rank consistency across different inputs, with certain directions consistently showing vanishing curvature. Leveraging this property, we identify a stable null space of the Hessian and then construct an additive weight transformation by linearly combining the vectors within this null space, thereby suppressing weight outliers without affecting the task loss. This additive transformation can be absorbed into the weights offline, requiring no inter-layer transformations and introducing no inference overhead. Moreover, the construction is efficiently achieved by a closed-form solution, without resource-intensive training or iterative procedures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OSAQ effectively suppresses outliers and enhances low-bit quantization performance. For instance, in 2-bit quantization, OSAQ, when integrated with GPTQ, achieves over 40% lower perplexity compared to vanilla GPTQ.

</details>


### [162] [AxMoE: Characterizing the Impact of Approximate Multipliers on Mixture-of-Experts DNN Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04754)
*Omkar B Shende,Marcello Traiola,Gayathri Ananthanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了近似乘法对混合专家（MoE）架构的影响，提出了AxMoE。实验表明，在无需重训练时，Dense基线对CNN最鲁棒；而在近似感知重训练后，不同架构和路由策略的恢复能力差异显著，其中Hard MoE在ViT-Small上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的DNN推理需要同时提升精度、计算效率和能效。近似计算和MoE架构分别被研究用于高效推理，但二者的交互作用此前完全未被探索。

Method: 提出了AxMoE，评估了三种MoE变体（Hard MoE、Soft MoE、Cluster MoE）与Dense基线在三种CNN架构（ResNet-20、VGG11_bn、VGG19_bn）和ViT-Small上的表现，使用EvoApproxLib库中的8种8位有符号乘法器（含一个精确基线）。

Result: 无重训练时，Dense基线对CNN最鲁棒；ViT-Small上所有拓扑退化速率相近。近似感知重训练后，ResNet-20完全恢复；VGG在中等乘法器下恢复，激进乘法器下除Cluster MoE on VGG11_bn外均不可逆失败；ViT-Small上Hard MoE在激进近似下优于Dense。

Conclusion: 这些结果为未来的近似MoE硬件-软件协同设计策略铺平了道路。

Abstract: Deep neural network (DNN) inference at the edge demands simultaneous improvements in accuracy, computational efficiency, and energy consumption. Approximate computing and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have each been studied as independent routes towards efficient inference, the former by replacing exact arithmetic with low-power approximate multipliers, the latter by routing inputs through specialized expert sub-networks to enable conditional computation. However, their interaction remains entirely unexplored. This paper presents AxMoE, the first study of the impact of approximate multiplication on MoE DNN architectures. We evaluate three MoE variants: Hard MoE, Soft MoE, and Cluster MoE against dense baselines across three CNN architectures (ResNet-20, VGG11_bn, VGG19_bn) on CIFAR-100 and a Vision Transformer (ViT-Small) on Tiny ImageNet-200 dataset, using eight 8-bit signed multipliers (including one exact baseline) from the EvoApproxLib library. Results show that, without retraining, the Dense baseline is the most resilient topology across all CNN architectures, whereas on ViT-Small, all topologies degrade at comparable rates regardless of routing strategy. After approximate-aware retraining, recovery varies substantially across architectures, topologies, and multipliers. ResNet-20 achieves full recovery across the entire multiplier range, whereas VGG architectures recover at moderate multipliers but fail irreversibly at aggressive ones for all topologies except Cluster MoE on VGG11_bn; on ViT-Small, Hard MoE outperforms Dense under aggressive approximation at equal normalized inference cost. These results pave the way for future approximate MoE hardware-software co-design strategies.

</details>


### [163] [Bilinear Mamba-Koopman Neural MPC for Varying Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04793)
*Matan Pagi,Zohar Sorek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Bilinear Mamba-Koopman Neural MPC，通过在潜在动态中引入控制依赖耦合，使算子能根据当前输入自适应调整，仅增加不到1%参数，在时变条件下显著提升了MPC的预测精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Koopman神经MPC模型假设系统算子与控制输入条件独立，这种条件独立约束限制了模型在单一MPC时域内适应动态变化的能力，尤其在时变条件和陈旧规划执行下表现不佳。

Method: 提出双线性Mamba-Koopman神经MPC，通过在潜在动态中引入控制依赖耦合，使有效算子能根据当前输入自适应调整；采用低秩结构（仅增加<1%参数），且能生成精确模型雅可比矩阵，支持高效序列凸规划（SCP）。

Result: 在CartPole和RSCP基准测试（时不变和时变）中，模型在预测精度上匹配或超越基线；在RSCP时变任务中提升显著，能稳定训练；在延迟重新规划实验中，双线性模型在陈旧规划执行下退化更平缓，鲁棒性优势明显。

Conclusion: 控制依赖的潜在动态为时变条件下鲁棒MPC提供了一种简单而有效的机制。

Abstract: Koopman-based neural MPC models generate time-varying dynamics from historical data, but preserve convexity by enforcing that the system operator is independent of the current control input. This conditional independence constraint limits adaptation to changing dynamics within a single MPC horizon, particularly under time-varying conditions and under stale-plan execution.
  We propose Bilinear Mamba-Koopman Neural MPC, a minimal extension that introduces control-dependent coupling in the latent dynamics, allowing the effective operator to adapt to the current input. The resulting model is a strict generalization of the standard linear, conditional-independence formulation, adds less than 1% parameters through a low-rank structure, and admits exact model Jacobians that enable efficient Sequential Convex Programming (SCP) with monotone-descent and KKT convergence results under standard trust-region assumptions.
  Across CartPole and RSCP benchmarks in time-invariant and time-varying regimes, the proposed model matches or improves forecasting accuracy on every cell when training noise is averaged out, with strict gains where control-state coupling is structurally present. Its main closed-loop gains appear in the RSCP TV task, where iterative SCP improves adaptation within the horizon and substantially stabilizes training; in CartPole TV, the gains are modest but consistent. In delayed re-planning experiments on the time-varying variants, the bilinear model degrades more gracefully under stale-plan execution, maintaining a consistent advantage on CartPole TV and a substantially larger robustness margin on RSCP TV. These results show that control-dependent latent dynamics provide a simple and effective mechanism for robust MPC under varying conditions.

</details>


### [164] [Unsat Core Prediction through Polarity-Aware Representation Learning over Clause-Literal Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04819)
*Zhenchao Sun,Shuai Ma,Ping Lu,Chongyang Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种极性感知的表示学习框架，通过子句-文字超图建模SAT公式，并引入极性感知分解机制，在多个SAT数据集上取得了有效结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将SAT公式建模为二部图或有向无环图，难以捕捉文字与子句之间的高阶交互，且无法建模SAT固有的极性相关特性（如正负文字的互补关系）。

Method: 1）将SAT公式建模为子句-文字超图并增广子句关联图以捕捉高阶结构交互；2）提出极性感知分解机制，将变量表示分离为极性不变分量和极性等变分量，显式建模正负文字关系；3）引入极性反转一致性正则化以增强训练中的极性一致性。

Result: 在多个SAT数据集上的实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过超图建模和极性感知机制，有效提升了SAT公式表示学习的能力，超越了现有方法的表达能力。

Abstract: Graph neural networks have been widely used in Boolean satisfiability (SAT) tasks to learn structural information from SAT formulas. The goal of these studies is to solve SAT instances or to enhance SAT solvers, including tasks such as unsat-core prediction. However, most existing approaches model a SAT formula as a bipartite graph or a directed acyclic graph, which are less expressive in capturing higher-order interactions among literals and clauses. Moreover, these approaches are limited in modeling intrinsic polarity-related properties of SAT, such as the complementary relationship between the positive and negative literals of a variable. To address these limitations, we propose a polarity-aware representation learning framework over clause-literal hypergraphs. We model SAT formulas as clause-literal hypergraphs augmented with a clause incidence graph to capture higher-order structural interactions. We then introduce a polarity-aware decomposed mechanism that separates variable representations into polarity invariant and equivariant components, explicitly modeling the relationship between positive and negative literals, with the resulting literal representations propagated along the hypergraph structure. We further incorporate a polarity-inversion consistency regularization to reinforce polarity-consistent representations during training. Experimental results on multiple SAT datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [165] [Improving FMQA via Initial Training Data Design Considering Marginal Bit Coverage in One-Hot Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04825)
*Taiga Hayashi,Yuya Seki,Kotaro Terada,Yosuke Mukasa,Shuta Kikuchi,Shu Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对FMQA中一位编码导致的稀疏初始化问题，提出通过LHS和Sobol'序列实现完全边际位覆盖，提升高维优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FMQA采用一位编码时，初始随机均匀采样会导致许多二元变量从未被激活，相应的FM参数得不到梯度更新，影响模型质量。

Method: 提出两种空间填充采样方法（LHS和Sobol'序列）构造初始训练数据，确保每位编码变量至少激活一次，形成LHS-FMQA和Sobol'-FMQA两种变体。

Result: 在17维和32维人力飞行器翼型优化基准上，两种方法均获得比基线FMQA更高的平均最终巡航速度，且32维问题优势更显著。

Conclusion: 通过空间填充采样确保完全边际位覆盖，能有效提升FMQA在高维黑箱优化中的性能。

Abstract: Factorization machine with quadratic-optimization annealing (FMQA) is a black-box optimization method that combines a factorization machine (FM) surrogate with QUBO-based search by an Ising machine. When FMQA is applied to integer or discretized continuous variables via one-hot encoding, uniform random initial sampling can leave many binary variables never active in the initial training data, and the corresponding FM parameters receive no direct gradient updates from the observed responses. We address this by designing the initial training data to achieve complete marginal bit coverage, namely, ensuring that every binary variable obtained by one-hot encoding takes the value one at least once. We use two space-filling sampling methods, Latin hypercube sampling (LHS) and the Sobol' sequence, yielding LHS-FMQA and Sobol'-FMQA. On the human-powered aircraft wing-shape optimization benchmark with 17 and 32 design variables, both proposed methods achieved numerically higher mean final cruising speeds than the baseline FMQA, with the advantage more pronounced on the 32-variable problem.

</details>


### [166] [Trustworthy Federated Label Distribution Learning under Annotation Quality Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04827)
*Junxiang Wu,Zhiqiang Kou,Hongwei Zeng,Wenke Huang,Biao Liu,Hanlin Gu,Yuheng Jia,Di Jiang,Yang Liu,Xin Geng,Qiang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中标签分布标注质量异构的问题，提出质量感知框架FedQual，包含自适应客户端训练与可靠性感知聚合机制，在四个新基准上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDL依赖高质量标签分布，获取成本高且易含噪声；联邦场景下数据隔离加剧了各客户端标注质量的异质性，导致基于样本量的传统聚合策略（如FedAvg）失效。

Method: 提出FedQual框架，包含两种耦合机制：(1) 质量自适应客户端训练，利用全局语义锚点校准低质量客户端并保持高质量客户端的自主性；(2) 可靠性感知服务器聚合，基于有效可靠信息而非原始样本量重新加权客户端贡献。

Result: 构建四个Fed-LDL基准数据集（FER-LDL、FI-LDL、PIPAL-LDL、KADID-LDL），在标注质量可控差异下进行实验，结果表明FedQual有效。

Conclusion: 在异构监督质量下，客户特定校准严格优于任何统一校准方法，FedQual在多个基准上表现优异。

Abstract: Label Distribution Learning (LDL) models supervision as an instance-wise probability distribution, enabling fine-grained learning under inherent ambiguity, but its success relies on high-fidelity label distributions that are costly to obtain and thus often noisy. Motivated by privacy-sensitive applications, we study Federated Label Distribution Learning (Fed-LDL), where data isolation further induces heterogeneous annotation quality across clients, making local updates unevenly reliable and breaking sample-size-based aggregation (e.g., FedAvg). To address this trust dilemma, we propose FedQual, a quality-aware Fed-LDL framework with two coupled mechanisms: (i) quality-adaptive client training guided by a global semantic anchor that calibrates low-quality clients while preserving high-quality autonomy, and (ii) reliability-aware server aggregation that reweights client contributions by effective reliable information rather than raw sample size. To enable rigorous evaluation, we construct four new Fed-LDL benchmarks (FER-LDL, FI-LDL, PIPAL-LDL, and KADID-LDL) with controlled annotation quality disparity. We further provide a theoretical guarantee showing that under heterogeneous supervision quality, client-specific calibration is strictly better than any uniform calibration. Extensive experiments on the proposed benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of FedQual.

</details>


### [167] [Concurrence of Symmetry Breaking and Nonlocality Phase Transitions in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04830)
*Yifan F. Zhang,Fangjun Hu,Guangkuo Liu,Mert Okyay,Xun Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散模型在生成动态中的相变现象，发现对称性破缺和非局域性两种临界时间在现代化扩散Transformer中几乎同时发生，首次统一了这两种相变概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成过程中存在一个关键的相变窗口，但目前存在两种互补的解释（对称性破缺视角和非局域性视角），尚不清楚这两种相变是否同时发生，需要统一理解。

Method: 通过评估现代化扩散Transformer的生成轨迹动态和结果，观察对称性破缺临界时间和非局域性临界时间的发生时刻，并进行对比分析。

Result: 发现两种临界时间在扩散Transformer中几乎同时发生，首次在实践中统一了两种相变概念。

Conclusion: 该发现为扩散模型何时以及为何依赖条件化和全局去噪提供了具体诊断，有助于评估模型效率并指导架构和采样方案的设计，避免不必要的计算。

Abstract: Diffusion models undergo a phase transition in a critical time window during generation dynamics, with two complementary diagnoses of criticality. The symmetry breaking picture views the critical window as when trajectories bifurcate into different semantic minima of the energy landscape, whereas the nonlocality picture views the critical window as when local denoising fails. We study whether two notions of such phase transitions are concurrent in modern diffusion transformers. By evaluating the dynamics and outcomes of the generation trajectory, we observe a near-simultaneous occurrence of the non-locality and symmetry breaking critical times. Our work is the first to unify the two notions of phase transitions in practice: it provides a concrete diagnostic for when and why diffusion models rely on conditioning and global denoising, enabling principled evaluation of model efficiency and guiding the design of architectures and sampling schemes that avoid unnecessary computation.

</details>


### [168] [Replay-Based Continual Learning for Physics-Informed Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04832)
*Yizheng Wang,Mohammad Sadegh Eshaghi,Xiaoying Zhuang,Timon Rabczuk,Yinghua Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于重放的持续学习方法，用于解决物理信息神经算子在分布外数据上的灾难性遗忘问题，在三个物理问题上验证了该方法能有效缓解遗忘、保持快速适应，并提升训练效率、降低内存和计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子方法在分布内数据上表现良好，但在分布外数据上性能显著下降，缺乏有效的持续学习能力来应对新数据分布。

Method: 采用基于重放的持续学习策略，结合蒸馏约束保留旧知识，并引入迁移学习LoRA实现快速适应新数据；方法完全基于物理信息驱动，无需标签数据。

Result: 在Darcy流、二维超弹性脑肿瘤和三维线性弹性TPMS三个物理问题上验证，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，保持了快速适应性，相比联合训练策略提升了训练效率并降低了内存和计算成本。

Conclusion: 所提方法为物理信息神经算子在持续学习场景下提供了一种高效、无需标签的解决方案，在保持性能的同时显著优化了资源消耗。

Abstract: Neural operators generally demonstrate strong predictive performance on in-distribution (ID) problems. However, a critical limitation of existing methods is their significant performance degradation when encountering out-of-distribution (OOD) data. To address this issue, this work introduces continual learning into physics-informed neural operators, with particular emphasis on neural operators built upon the Transolver architecture, and proposes a simple yet effective replay-based continual learning strategy. The proposed method is fully physics-informed and does not require labeled data, relying solely on input fields together with physical constraints for training. When new OOD data become available, a small number of past data are incorporated through a distillation-based constraint to preserve previously acquired knowledge and alleviate catastrophic forgetting. Meanwhile, a transfer learning LoRA is employed to enable rapid adaptation to the new data. The proposed framework is systematically validated on three representative physical problems, including the Darcy flow problem in fluid mechanics, a two-dimensional hyperelastic brain tumor problem in biomechanics, and a three-dimensional linear elastic Triply Periodic Minimal Surfaces problem in solid mechanics. The results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting on previously learned data while maintaining fast adaptability to new data. Compared with conventional joint training strategies, the proposed method significantly improves training efficiency while reducing additional memory usage and computational cost.

</details>


### [169] [Bridging Input Feature Spaces Towards Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04834)
*Moshe Eliasof,Krishna Sri Ipsit Mantri,Beatrice Bevilacqua,Bruno Ribeiro,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ALL-IN方法，通过将节点特征投影到共享随机空间并利用协方差统计构建表征，实现图模型在不同数据集间的可迁移性，无需重新训练或修改架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图学习缺乏统一的输入空间，不同数据集的输入特征在语义、取值范围和维度上存在差异，阻碍了图模型作为基础模型跨数据集的泛化能力。

Method: 将节点特征投影到共享随机空间，通过协方差统计构建表征，消除对原始特征空间的依赖；理论上证明了节点-协方差算子及节点表征对输入特征排列和正交变换的不变性。

Result: 在多种节点级和图级任务上，ALL-IN在未见过的数据集上取得强劲性能，无需改变架构或重新训练。

Conclusion: ALL-IN为输入无关、可迁移的图模型提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Unlike vision and language domains, graph learning lacks a shared input space, as input features differ across graph datasets not only in semantics, but also in value ranges and dimensionality. This misalignment prevents graph models from generalizing across datasets, limiting their use as foundation models. In this work, we propose ALL-IN, a simple and theoretically grounded method that enables transferability across datasets with different input features. Our approach projects node features into a shared random space and constructs representations via covariance-based statistics, thus eliminating dependence on the original feature space. We show that the computed node-covariance operators and the resulting node representations are invariant in distribution to permutations of the input features. We further demonstrate that the expected operator exhibits invariance to general orthogonal transformations of the input features. Empirically, ALL-IN achieves strong performance across diverse node- and graph-level tasks on unseen datasets with new input features, without requiring architecture changes or retraining. These results point to a promising direction for input-agnostic, transferable graph models.

</details>


### [170] [Self-Attention as Transport: Limits of Symmetric Spectral Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04893)
*Dominik Dahlem,Diego Maniloff,Mac Misiura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对注意力机制中对称谱分析的方法在检测信息流方向上是盲视的，并提出不对称系数G作为方向唯一控制参数。通过二分Cheeger界分析，发现均匀因果注意力有n无关的界φ≥1/5，窗口注意力可突破此界。建立φ（容量）和G（方向）的双轴诊断框架，在多个模型上验证了可解释信号（LC-AUROC 0.62-0.84），并在HaluEval和MedHallu上观察到极性反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谱分析方法仅能捕捉注意力的对称结构，无法区分信息流动方向，导致对幻觉模式的诊断存在盲区。需要建立能同时刻画容量和方向的双轴诊断框架。

Method: 理论证明所有转置不变的谱诊断都是结构上方向盲视的，推导不对称系数G作为方向唯一控制参数。结合闭式二分Cheeger界分析因果注意力和窗口注意力的传输容量边界。在多种LLM（最高8B参数）上进行长度控制实验验证。

Result: 均匀因果注意力满足φ≥1/5的n无关下界，最差切割在t*/n≈0.32处；窗口注意力可突破下界至O(w/n)。双轴诊断在LC-AUROC上达到0.62-0.84，HaluEval和MedHallu上极性反转。

Conclusion: 注意力幻觉的失败模式在形状上有本质差异（不仅是数值差异），提出的双轴诊断框架能够有效区分不同幻觉类型，且极性预测可证伪。

Abstract: Large language models hallucinate in predictable ways: attention routing fails by over-concentrating on a narrow set of positions, or by spreading so diffusely that relevance is diluted, and the shape of the failure carries diagnostic signal. A widely used family of spectral methods analyzes the symmetric component of the degree-normalized attention operator, which governs transport capacity; we prove that every transpose-invariant spectral diagnostic of this operator is structurally orientation-blind (it cannot distinguish an operator from its transpose, and therefore cannot detect information-flow direction), with a quantitative converse establishing the asymmetry coefficient $G$ as the unique control parameter for direction.
  Pairing this with a closed-form bipartite-Cheeger landscape for canonical causal architectures, we show that uniform causal attention satisfies an $n$-independent floor $φ\ge 1/5$ with worst cut at $t^\ast/n \approx 0.32$, while window attention pierces the floor as $O(w/n)$; failure modes are shape-different, not just value-different. The resulting two-axis diagnostic ($φ$ for capacity, $G$ for direction) yields a falsifiable polarity prediction: bottleneck- and diffuse-dominated benchmarks should exhibit opposite polarity. Under length-controlled evaluation, transport features retain interpretable signal (LC-AUROC from 0.62 to 0.84) on tested models up to 8B parameters, with polarity reversing as predicted between HaluEval and MedHallu.

</details>


### [171] [Regime-Conditioned Evaluation in Multi-Context Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04895)
*Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了迁移贝叶斯优化（transfer-BO）中一个被广泛忽视的问题：算法排名会随预算/搜索空间比（B/|A|）和先验质量（rho）而逆转。作者提出了便携式制度评分（PRS），并通过大量实验证明，无条件排名不稳定，而PRS能有效预测条件优势。最终提出RegimePlanner自适应切换采集策略，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有transfer-BO比较文献通常只报告平均处理效应（ATE），忽略了不同先验质量、预算比例和度量条件下的条件效应差异，导致排名不稳定且对实践者缺乏指导意义。

Method: 对40篇transfer-BO论文进行审计；提出PRS=(B/|A|)(1-rho)指标；构建层次模型分析79种条件；提出RegimePlanner在线估计rho并自适应切换采集策略；进行预注册预测验证。

Result: 98%的论文未将B/|A|作为控制轴；改变预算即能逆转排名；层次模型beta=0.50 (p=1.1e-9)；19%的条件落入等价区；5个已发表反转案例中PRS能预测胜者；RegimePlanner全部16个HPO-B搜索空间获胜，GDSC2超过oracle 18%；预注册预测准确率67.5%，EMA先验族内超90%。

Conclusion: 无条件排名不可靠，应报告B/|A|、rho、K和度量等上下文参数；PRS和RegimePlanner提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Published transfer-BO comparisons often estimate an average treatment effect of acquisition choice over hidden regime variables, while practitioners need the conditional effect for their specific prior quality, budget ratio, and metric. An audit of 40 transfer-BO papers from NeurIPS, ICML, ICLR, AISTATS, UAI, TMLR, JMLR, and AutoML-Conf (2022-2025) finds that 98% never vary B/|A| as a controlled axis. On the same GDSC2 benchmark, changing only the budget reverses the ranking: at B=50, Greedy outperforms UCB by 0.050 Hit@1, while at B=100, UCB outperforms Greedy by 0.035. We capture this transition with the Portable Regime Score PRS=(B/|A|)(1-rho), where rho is the prior rank correlation and can be estimated from pilot contexts before the main comparison. Across 79 conditions spanning chemistry, drug-response biology, and HPO, a hierarchical model gives beta=0.50 (p=1.1e-9), and 19% of conditions fall in an equivalence zone where |advantage|<0.01 Hit@1. In five published reversal cases, PRS predicts the winner from pre-comparison observables. A No-Free-Leaderboard proposition explains why unconditional rankings are unstable: when CATE changes sign across regimes, the reported ATE becomes a function of benchmark mixture. RegimePlanner, which estimates rho online and switches acquisition accordingly, wins all 16 HPO-B search spaces at B=100 and exceeds the matched {Greedy,UCB} per-context oracle on GDSC2 by 18%. Pre-registered predictions achieve 27/40=67.5% overall accuracy and above 90% within EMA prior families. The practical protocol is simple: report B/|A|, rho, K, and metric alongside any claimed acquisition advantage.

</details>


### [172] [A geometric relation of the error introduced by sampling a language model's output distribution to its internal state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04899)
*Albert F. Modenbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现GPT风格语言模型在预测概率分布分散时对单Token变化敏感，并从几何角度推导出仅依赖于Token嵌入几何的$\mathfrak{so}(n)$值1-形式，其曲率具有语义意义，能与世界模型耦合，反映模型内部问题表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解GPT风格语言模型在概率分布分散的生成点对单Token变化敏感的原因，并探索这种敏感性与模型内部表征之间的几何关联。

Method: 将模型对单Token变化的敏感性视为几何性质，推导出仅依赖于Token嵌入几何的$\mathfrak{so}(n)$值1-形式，并分析其曲率。

Result: 曲率在象棋推理任务中具有语义意义，能与现成指令微调模型的世界模型耦合，变换按棋盘区域聚类，并遵循棋子重要性。

Conclusion: Token空间的几何直接反映了模型如何内部表征问题。

Abstract: GPT-style language models are sensitive to single-token changes at generation points where the predicted probability distribution is spread across multiple tokens. Viewing this sensitivity as a geometric property, we derive an $\mathfrak{so}(n)$-valued 1-form that depends only on the geometry of the token embeddings. Despite this purely geometric origin, we show that its curvature is semantically meaningful: On chess reasoning tasks, the curvature couples to the world model of an off-the-shelf instruction-tuned model, with transformations clustering by board region and respecting piece importance. Our findings suggest that token space geometry directly reflects how models internally represent problems.

</details>


### [173] [Breaking the Quality-Privacy Tradeoff in Tabular Data Generation via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04911)
*Xinyan Han,Yan Lu,Xiaoyu Lin,Yuanyuan Jiang,Yuanrui Wang,Xuanyue Li,Wenchao Zou,Xingxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffICL将通过上下文学习从大量数据集中学到的结构先验用于小样本表格数据生成，从而在不牺牲隐私的前提下提升数据质量，打破了传统模型在少量数据场景下的质量-隐私权衡困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格生成模型在小数据量场景下存在数据质量与隐私保护之间的明显权衡：提升数据质量往往会导致对训练样本的过度记忆，从而削弱隐私保护。这是因为小训练集难以让模型区分可泛化的结构与样本特定模式。

Method: 提出DiffICL框架，将表格数据生成转化为上下文学习问题。不从头拟合每个数据集，而是利用从大量数据集中预训练获得的先验结构知识，从有限的上下文样本中推断数据分布，而非记忆单个样本。

Result: 在14个真实数据集上的实验表明，DiffICL同时提升了数据质量和隐私保护，且生成的合成数据可有效用于数据增强。

Conclusion: 通过更好的训练范式（上下文学习），可以有效改善表格数据生成中的质量-隐私权衡问题。

Abstract: Tabular data synthesis aims to generate high-quality data while preserving privacy. However, we find that existing tabular generative models exhibit a clear tradeoff in the small-data regime: improving data quality typically comes at the cost of increased memorization of training samples, thereby weakening privacy protection. This tradeoff arises because small training sets make it difficult for dataset-specific generative models to distinguish generalizable structure from sample-specific patterns. To address this, we propose DiffICL, which formulates tabular data generation as an in-context learning problem. Instead of fitting each dataset from scratch,DiffICL leverages pretrained structural priors learned from a large collection of datasets, enabling it to infer data distributions from limited context rather than memorizing individual samples. We evaluate DiffICL on 14 real-world datasets. Results show that DiffICL improves both data quality and privacy, and generate synthetic data that provides effective data augmentation. Our findings suggest that the quality-privacy tradeoff can be improved through better training paradigms.

</details>


### [174] [Koopman Identification of Nonlinear Systems via Reservoir Liftings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04917)
*Weibin Gu,Chen Yang,Lu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RC-Koopman框架，利用储层计算作为状态依赖的有限维Koopman字典，通过回声状态属性保证数值稳定性，并基于相关系数的谱半径选择算法匹配储层记忆与系统时间尺度，在非线性动力系统重建精度与稳定性之间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Koopman算子理论在学习非线性动力系统的可解线性表示时，面临字典选择困难、时间记忆编码问题以及数值病态条件等挑战。

Method: 借鉴储层计算范式，将储层解释为具有状态记忆的有限维Koopman字典，通过谱半径显式控制时间深度；利用回声状态属性保证适定性和良好数值条件；提出基于相关系数的谱半径选择算法，对齐储层记忆与主导系统时间尺度。

Result: 在合成基准测试中，与EDMD和基于Hankel的提升方法相比，RC-Koopman在非线性动力学的重建精度和动态稳定性之间实现了更优的平衡。

Conclusion: 储层的有限记忆决定了从提升特征中可观测到的Koopman特征函数；该方法为非线性动力系统的线性表示学习提供了实用且数值稳定的新途径。

Abstract: Learning tractable linear representations of nonlinear dynamical systems via Koopman operator theory is often hindered by dictionary selection, temporal memory encoding, and numerical ill-conditioning. Inspired by Reservoir Computing (RC) paradigm, this paper introduces the RC-Koopman framework, which interprets reservoir as a stateful, finite-dimensional Koopman dictionary whose temporal depth is explicitly controlled by its spectral radius. We show that the Echo State Property (ESP) guarantees well-posedness and favorable numerical conditioning of the lifted Koopman approximation. A correlation-based spectral radius selection algorithm aligns reservoir memory with dominant system timescales. Analysis reveals how the finite memory of the reservoir determines which Koopman eigenfunctions remain observable from the lifted features. Evaluation on synthetic benchmarks demonstrates that RC-Koopman achieves a favorable balance between reconstruction accuracy of the underlying nonlinear dynamics and dynamical stability, compared to Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (EDMD) and Hankel-based lifting approaches. Code available at: https://github.com/NEAR-the-future/RC-Koopman.git

</details>


### [175] [Reinforcement Learning for Compositional Generalization with Outcome-Level Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04920)
*Xiyan Fu,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过结果级强化学习（GRPO算法）提升组合泛化能力，相比监督微调，强化学习能重塑输出分布，减少对训练常见组合的过拟合，在多个基准上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调（SFT）在词元级别的训练范式未能捕捉全局组合结构，导致模型难以泛化到未见过的组合，因此探索强化学习作为替代方案。

Method: 采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 进行结果级强化学习，对比了简单二元结果奖励和包含组合反馈的复合奖励两种策略。

Result: 在多个组合泛化基准上，强化学习比监督微调取得了更好的组合泛化性能。

Conclusion: 强化学习通过重塑输出分布、减少对训练常见组合的过拟合来改善组合泛化，尤其对复杂组合类型效果显著。

Abstract: Compositional generalization refers to correctly interpret novel combinations of known primitives, which remains a major challenge. Existing approaches often rely on supervised fine-tuning, which encourages models to imitate target outputs. This token-level training paradigm fails to capture the global compositional structure required for generalizing to unseen combinations. In this work, we investigate whether compositional generalization can instead be improved through outcome-level reinforcement learning. We adopt Group Relative Policy Optimization to optimize models based on feedback on their final outputs. Within this framework, we explore both a simple binary outcome reward and a composite reward that provides additional composition feedback. Experiments on multiple compositional benchmarks show that reinforcement learning improves compositional generalization compared to supervised fine-tuning. Further analysis reveals that supervised models tend to overfit frequent training compositions, whereas reinforcement learning improves compositional generalization by reshaping the output distribution, particularly for more complex composition types.

</details>


### [176] [Adaptive Inverted-Index Routing for Granular Mixtures-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04952)
*Klaus-Rudolf Kladny,Maximilian Mordig,Bernhard Schölkopf,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIR-MoE提出了一种基于向量量化的自适应倒排索引路由架构，通过两阶段路由（粗筛选+精细打分）在细粒度MoE模型中高效近似top-k路由，无需修改模型结构或损失函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度MoE（大量小专家）提升性能，但传统路由需要对所有专家评分，计算成本急剧增加，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 两阶段路由：(1) 将token分配给VQ码字构建候选专家集；(2) 仅对候选集进行精细评分，近似top-k路由。基于倒排索引思想，无需修改模型架构或损失函数。

Result: AIR-MoE在细粒度MoE场景下相比现有路由方法取得了更优的性能，并提供了召回率的下界理论保证。

Conclusion: AIR-MoE是一种高效且即插即用的路由方案，在细粒度MoE中平衡了计算效率与路由质量，具有良好的理论保障和实际效果。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models enable scalable transformer architectures by activating only a subset of experts per token. Recent evidence suggests that performance improves with increasingly granular experts, i.e., many small experts instead of a few large ones. However, this regime substantially increases routing cost, which can dominate computation. We introduce adaptive inverted-index routing for MoE (AIR-MoE), an inverted-index-inspired routing architecture based on vector quantization (VQ). In a first stage, AIR-MoE performs coarse shortlisting by assigning tokens to VQ codewords to construct a candidate set of experts. In a second stage, fine scoring computes exact routing scores restricted to this shortlist. This two-stage procedure approximates true top-k routing while avoiding full expert scoring and, in contrast to prior work, imposing no structural constraints on expert parameters. AIR-MoE serves as a drop-in replacement for standard routers and requires no modifications to the model architecture or loss function. We further provide a lower bound on the mass recall achieved by AIR-MoE that yields insights into its inner workings. Empirically, we demonstrate that AIR-MoE achieves improved performance compared to existing routing approaches in granular MoE settings.

</details>


### [177] [Order-based Rehearsal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04955)
*Yu-Xuan Tao,Tian-Zuo Wang,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于序结构的避免不良未来（AUF）决策方法，证明序结构在无需完整图结构的情况下也能有效进行AUF决策，并提出信息论序学习方法和基于序的AUF优化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AUF方法依赖图结构，但图结构从观测数据中学习困难且估计误差大。本文旨在探索序结构是否足以替代图结构进行AUF决策。

Method: 1) 提出信息论序学习方法，不限制结构函数形式和噪声分布类型；2) 构建基于序的采样器近似决策影响；3) 结合替代目标最大化决策后成功概率，将AUF任务转化为可微优化问题。

Result: 序学习方法优于现有方法；AUF方法不仅超越依赖学习图或学习序的方法，而且匹配甚至超越使用真实图结构的Oracle基线。

Conclusion: 序结构足以支撑AUF决策，学习完整图结构并非总是必要。序结构在信息量较少的情况下，仍能有效识别决策影响。

Abstract: When a machine learning (ML) model forecasts an undesired event, one often seeks a decision to avoid it, known as the avoiding undesired future (AUF) problem. Many rehearsal learning methods have been proposed for AUF, but they rely on an underlying graph structure; learning such a graph from observational data is challenging and can incur substantial estimation error. In this work, we demonstrate that the order structure can be sufficient for AUF decision-making, and propose the first order-based rehearsal learning method. Although an order is less informative than a graph, it can be sufficient to identify the influence of decisions from observational data, suggesting that learning the entire graph is not always necessary. To learn the order, we develop an information-theoretic method that imposes no restrictions on the form of structural functions or the type of noise distributions. For AUF decision-making, we construct an order-based sampler to approximate the influence of decisions and, combined with a surrogate objective for maximizing the post-decision success probability, reduce the AUF task to a differentiable optimization problem. Experiments show that our order learning method outperforms existing methods, and that our AUF approach not only surpasses methods relying on learned graphs or learned orders, but also matches or even exceeds oracle baselines that are given the true graph.

</details>


### [178] [KernelBench-X: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM-Generated GPU Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04956)
*Han Wang,Jintao Zhang,Kai Jiang,Haoxu Wang,Jianfei Chen,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KernelBench-X是一个面向LLM生成Triton kernel的基准测试，包含15个类别176个任务。研究发现：任务结构比方法设计更能决定正确性；迭代改进能提升编译率但会降低性能；正确的kernel中46.6%比PyTorch基线更慢，量化任务完全失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答一个根本性的实证问题：基于LLM的Triton kernel生成能力在何处失效，以及为什么会失效。

Method: 构建KernelBench-X基准，包含15个类别176个任务，对5种代表性方法进行类别感知的正确性和硬件效率系统比较。

Result: 1) 任务结构（类别）解释的正确性方差（9.4%）远大于方法设计（3.3%）；2) 迭代改进使编译率从52.3%升至68.8%，但平均加速比从1.58×降至1.44×；3) 46.6%的正确kernel比PyTorch基线慢，跨硬件速度差异达21.4×，量化任务0/30成功。

Conclusion: 未来进展需关注全局协调、显式建模数值精度，以及将硬件效率纳入生成过程。

Abstract: LLM-based Triton kernel generation has attracted significant interest, yet a fundamental empirical question remains unanswered: where does this capability break down, and why? We present KernelBench-X, a benchmark designed to answer this question through category-aware evaluation of correctness and hardware efficiency across 176 tasks in 15 categories. Our systematic comparison of five representative methods yields three main findings. First, task structure determines correctness more than method design. Category explains nearly three times more variance in semantic correctness than method (9.4% vs 3.3% explained deviance), and 72% of Fusion tasks fail across all five methods while Math tasks are solved consistently. Second, iterative refinement improves correctness, but not performance. Across GEAK iterations, compile rate rises from 52.3% to 68.8% while average speedup declines from $1.58\times$ to $1.44\times$; newly rescued kernels consistently underperform persistently correct ones ($1.16\times$ vs $1.58\times$ speedup in round~0$\to$1). Third, correctness does not imply efficiency. 46.6% of correct kernels are slower than the PyTorch eager baseline, and cross-hardware speedup variance reaches $21.4\times$. Besides, quantization remains completely unsolved (0/30 successes) despite non-trivial compilation rates, revealing systematic misunderstanding of numerical computation contracts rather than surface-level syntax errors. These findings suggest that future progress depends on handling global coordination, explicitly modeling numerical precision, and incorporating hardware efficiency into generation. The code is available at https://github.com/BonnieW05/KernelBenchX

</details>


### [179] [Delving into Non-Exchangeability for Conformal Prediction in Graph-Structured Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04957)
*Ruichao Guo,Xingyao Han,Luo Wenshui,Zhe Liu,Chen Gong,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出频谱图条件可交换性（SGCE）概念，并基于此设计SCALE方法，利用图小波分解高低频分量，对高频残差进行共形预测，在交通数据集上实现有效覆盖并改善覆盖-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构多变量时间序列的点预测缺乏严格的量化不确定性方法，尽管共形预测（CP）理论上可以提供覆盖保证，但跨节点耦合破坏了数据可交换性假设，导致直接应用CP不可靠。

Method: 提出频谱图条件可交换性（SGCE），将可交换的高频分量条件化于低频分量以保留全局趋势；基于SGCE设计SCALE方法，使用图小波分解高低频，并通过低频嵌入的自适应门控对高频残差进行共形化。

Result: 在真实交通数据集上，SCALE不仅实现了有效的覆盖保证，而且持续改善了与现有最优CP方法相比的覆盖-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 频谱域中的条件共形预测能有效处理图时间序列中的跨节点耦合问题，在保证覆盖的同时提升效率，为图结构时间序列的不确定性量化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Point forecasting for graph-structured multivariate time series is a fundamental problem, but rigorous uncertainty quantification for such predictions is still underexplored. Conformal prediction (CP) offers uncertainty estimation with a solid coverage guarantee under the exchangeability assumption, which requires the joint data distribution to be unchanged under permutation. However, in graph-structured time series, inherent cross-node coupling can violate the exchangeability condition, making direct application of CP unreliable. Inspired by the spectral graph theory, such coupling resides in global trends and can be characterized by the low-frequency components, while high-frequency components are nearly exchangeable. Therefore, we propose a novel concept named Spectral Graph Conditional Exchangeability (SGCE), which conditions exchangeable high-frequency components on low-frequency ones to preserve global trends and enable effective CP in the spectral domain. Based on SGCE, we further propose Spectral Conformal prediction via wAveLEt transform (SCALE). SCALE uses graph wavelets to decompose low/high-frequency components and conformalizes high-frequency residuals via adaptive gating over a low-frequency embedding. Experimental results on real-world traffic datasets show that SCALE not only achieves valid coverage but also consistently improves the coverage-efficiency trade-off over the state-of-the-art CP methods.

</details>


### [180] [Conceptors for Semantic Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04980)
*Ilias Triantafyllopoulos,Young-Min Cho,Ren Tao,Miranda Muqing Miao,Sunny Rai,Lyle Ungar,Sharath Chandra Guntuku,Neville Ryant,João Sedoc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Conceptor方法，使用软投影矩阵代替单一方向向量进行语言模型行为控制，在几何上保留了概念的多维子空间，并通过布尔代数实现组合性，在多个评估维度上优于或匹配基线方法，同时产生更少的退化输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于激活的LLM行为控制方法将每个概念简化为单一方向向量，忽略了其几何结构的多维性，缺乏对概念子空间的深入理解。

Method: 使用Conceptor（软投影矩阵）从双极概念两端的激活中估计，保留概念的全维度子空间；利用Conceptor quota作为无参数层选择诊断工具；通过闭式布尔代数实现概念组合（AND, OR, NOT）。

Result: Conceptor quota在三个指令微调模型和三个语义维度上预测概念可分性，Pearson相关系数高达0.96；在多维概念子空间的层上，Conceptor匹配或优于加性基线，且产生显著更少的退化输出。

Conclusion: Conceptor在几何上更严谨，支持组合操作，且在实践中比基于单一方向的对比对方法更安全、更有效。

Abstract: Activation-based steering provides control of LLM behavior at inference time, but the dominant paradigm reduces each concept to a single direction whose geometry is left largely unexamined. Rather than selecting a single steering direction, we use conceptors: soft projection matrices estimated from activations pooled across both poles of a bipolar concept, which preserve the concept's full multidimensional subspace. A geometric analysis shows the bipolar subspace strictly subsumes the single-vector baseline. We further show that the conceptor quota provides a parameter-free layer-selection diagnostic, predicting concept separability with Pearson correlations up to r=0.96 across three instruction-tuned models and three semantic dimensions. Beyond selection, conceptors admit a closed-form Boolean algebra (AND, OR, NOT): we evaluate conceptor compositionality on thematically related sub-concepts. Across a systematic five-axis design-space evaluation, conceptors match or outperform additive baselines at layers where concept subspaces are multi-dimensional while producing substantially fewer degenerate outputs. Conceptor steering is a geometrically principled, compositional, and practically safer alternative to single-direction steering from a limited number of contrastive pairs.

</details>


### [181] [Self-Induced Outcome Potential: Turn-Level Credit Assignment for Agents without Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04984)
*Senkang Hu,Yong Dai,Xudong Han,Zhengru Fang,Yuzhi Zhao,Sam Tak Wu Kwong,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIOP是一种基于语义聚类和潜在未来状态的自激励信号方法，用于为长程LLM智能体的中间信息收集回合分配信用，无需过程级奖励或任务特定验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM智能体依赖中间信息收集回合，但训练反馈通常仅在最终答案处提供，过程级奖励需要高质量人工标注，现有方法要么需要答案监督或任务特定验证器，要么无法为中间回合分配信用。

Method: 对每个查询采样多条轨迹，将最终答案聚类为语义结果模式，构建可靠性感知的目标分布，然后奖励那些增加对未来可靠状态后验支持的中间回合，使用可解的聚类级近似。

Result: 在七个搜索增强的智能体推理基准上，SIOP在无需验证器的条件下平均性能优于结果级基线，并接近有金答案监督的结果基线。

Conclusion: SIOP将信息势能塑造从金答案监督泛化到无任务特定验证器的场景，同时避免了标准GRPO使用的广播式轨迹级优势。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents depend on intermediate information-gathering turns, yet training feedback is usually observed only at the final answer, because process-level rewards require high-quality human annotation. Existing turn-level shaping methods reward turns that increase the likelihood of a gold answer, but they require answer supervision or stable task-specific verifiers. Conversely, label-free RL methods extract self-signals from output distributions, but mainly at the answer or trajectory level and therefore cannot assign credit to intermediate turns. We propose Self-Induced Outcome Potential (SIOP), which treats semantic clusters of final answers as latent future outcome states for potential-based turn-level credit assignment. For each query, SIOP samples multiple rollouts, clusters final answers into semantic outcome modes, and builds a reliability-aware target distribution over these states. It then rewards turns for increasing posterior support for reliable future states using a tractable cluster-level approximation. The objective generalizes information-potential shaping from gold-answer supervision to settings without task-specific gold verifiers while avoiding the broadcasted rollout-level advantages used by standard GRPO. We formalize the framework, characterize its supervised gold-answer limit, and show that SIOP improves average performance over verifier-free outcome-level baselines on seven search-augmented agentic reasoning benchmarks while approaching a gold-supervised outcome baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/dl-m9/SIOP.git.

</details>


### [182] [DualTCN: A Physics-Constrained Temporal Convolutional Network for 2 Time-Domain Marine CSEM Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04997)
*Khaled Ahmed,Ghada Omar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DualTCN是首个用于海洋可控源电磁（MCSEM）时域数据反演的深度学习框架，通过TCN编码器与软阶梯解码器，实现了对地下电导率-深度剖面的快速、高精度重建，显著优于传统优化方法，并具备不确定性量化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MCSEM反演依赖局部优化方法（如Levenberg-Marquardt），计算成本高且易陷入局部最优。现有深度学习方法多针对频域或假设离散化网格，缺乏直接对时域瞬态数据进行高效反演的框架。DualTCN旨在突破这一瓶颈，提供更快速、鲁棒且可量化不确定性的反演方案。

Method: DualTCN采用TCN编码器+晚期时间分支+辅助海底深度头的架构（379K参数），直接回归四个地球模型参数（σ₁, σ₂, d₁, d₂），并通过可微分的软阶梯解码器重构电导率-深度剖面。训练中引入基于课程学习的幅度增强策略提升噪声鲁棒性，并通过MC Dropout实现不确定性量化。

Result: DualTCN在A100 GPU上单样本反演仅需3.5ms，损失较基线降低25.3%，σ₂预测R²达0.898。在±2%随机幅度误差下，平均R²为0.858（无增强仅0.363）。对比传统方法（LM、L-BFGS-B），平均R²=0.877 vs 0.129-0.439，计算成本降低至1/21,000。薄层分辨率为物理极限（R²≈0.23），但对三层扩展模型仍有效（基底电导率R²≈0.88）。不确定性量化方面，σ₁校准良好（PICP90=0.944），d₂欠覆盖（PICP90=0.572），可通过温度缩放或分裂共形预测改善。

Conclusion: DualTCN为MCSEM时域数据反演提供了首个高效的深度学习解决方案，在速度、精度和鲁棒性上全面超越传统方法，并具备不确定性量化能力，尽管薄层分辨率和短偏移距信号限制仍有改进空间。

Abstract: DualTCN is the first deep-learning framework for inverting time-domain marine controlled-source electromagnetic (MCSEM) transient data. Moving away from traditional subsurface discretization, the framework regresses four earth-model parameters -- $σ_1$, $σ_2$, $d_1$, $d_2$ -- and reconstructs conductivity-depth profiles using a differentiable soft-step decoder. The optimized architecture (379K parameters) features a Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) encoder paired with a late-time branch and an auxiliary seafloor-depth head. This design achieves a 25.3\% loss reduction over baseline models, with high predictive accuracy ($R^2 = 0.898$ for $σ_2$) and an inversion speed of 3.5~ms per sample on an A100 GPU.
  The framework demonstrates high robustness to noise through curriculum-based amplitude augmentation, maintaining a mean $\bar{R}^2$ of 0.858 at $\pm2\%$ random amplitude error, compared to $0.363$ without augmentation. DualTCN generalizes effectively to three-layer extensions (seawater/resistive layer/basement), accurately resolving basement conductivity ($R^2 \approx 0.88$), though thin-layer resolution remains a physical limitation ($R^2 \approx 0.23$).
  In comparative benchmarks, DualTCN significantly outperforms traditional local optimization methods like Levenberg-Marquardt and L-BFGS-B, yielding a mean $\bar{R}^2 = 0.877$ versus 0.129-0.439 for multi-start baselines, while operating at up to 21,000$\times$ lower computational cost. Finally, the framework incorporates uncertainty quantification via Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout. While well-calibrated for $σ_1$ (PICP90 = 0.944), inherent signal limitations at short offsets (200m) lead to under-coverage for $d_2$ (PICP90 = 0.572), which can be mitigated through post-hoc temperature scaling or split conformal prediction.

</details>


### [183] [Learned Neighbor Trust for Collaborative Deployment in Model-Agnostic Decentralized Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05009)
*Michael Lanier,Luise Ge,Sastry Kompella,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LNTrust方法，让分布式节点在训练时学习信任函数，从而在推理时动态选择邻居进行协作，显著提升部署精度并降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式蒸馏方法仅在训练时协调，推理时各节点孤立运行，忽略了推理时仍可用的强大邻居。在异构、数据稀缺的IoT场景中，这种设计是不完整的。

Method: 提出Learned Neighbor Trust (LNTrust)，每个节点基于本地验证集学习一个紧凑的信任函数。该函数在训练时控制辅助蒸馏，在推理时定义部署集成，实现训练到推理的无缝协作迁移。

Result: 在多个数据集和拓扑结构上，LNTrust相比最强输出基线大幅提升部署准确率，且通信开销显著低于先前方法。

Conclusion: 让节点学会何时信任邻居，能够将训练时的协作有效迁移到推理阶段，实现更好的异构分布式部署效果。

Abstract: Many decentralized distillation methods are designed around training-time coordination, yet deploy each node in isolation even when more capable neighbors remain available at inference time. This is an incomplete objective for settings such as IoT, where devices are heterogeneous, data is scarce and skewed, and a node's strongest neighbors may far exceed its own local capacity. We study how nodes should train so that their predictions compose well at deployment, and how each node should learn whom to trust. Under a server-free, model-agnostic protocol where nodes exchange only queries and soft predictions, we propose Learned Neighbor Trust (LNTrust) wherein each node learns a compact trust function over its neighborhood from local validation evidence. This trust function gates auxiliary distillation during training and defines a deployment ensemble at inference, so that collaboration learned during training transfers directly to deployment. Across datasets and topologies, LNTrust improves deployed accuracy over the strongest output-only baseline by large margins while using significantly less communication than previous methods.

</details>


### [184] [Graph-SND: Sparse Aggregation for Behavioral Diversity in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05020)
*Shawn Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Graph-SND，通过将SND的完全图平均替换为任意图上的加权平均，将计算复杂度从O(n²)降至O(|E|)，在保持语义不变的同时实现了约10倍的加速，并通过理论分析和实验验证了其无偏性、集中性以及在多样性控制中的应用效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始SND（系统神经多样性）度量每次计算需要对所有$\binom{n}{2}$个智能体对进行距离计算，复杂度为二次方，严重限制了其在大型多智能体系统中的应用。因此需要一种能保持度量语义但计算更高效的方法。

Method: 将完全图上的平均距离替换为任意图$G$的边上的加权平均。三种情况：$G=K_n$恢复原SND；固定稀疏图得到局部多样性度量（O(|E|)代价）；随机边采样得到无偏Horvitz-Thompson估计量。理论分析了扩展图的前向索引失真界和低秩距离结构下的谱改进，以及随机d-正则图的概率界。

Result: 在VMAS上验证了恢复性、无偏性、集中性和加速效果。n=100的PPO实验中，伯努利-0.1采样实现约10倍加速；n=500时GPU时间符合预期的$\binom{n}{2}/|E|$加速比；随机d-正则扩展图在Θ(n log n)边数下SND比值在[0.9987,1.0013]之间；n=50的DiCo多样性控制中cut约9.5倍代价。

Conclusion: SND的聚合瓶颈可以在不改变度量语义的情况下被消除，Graph-SND是一种即插即用的稀疏替代方案，能够扩展到完全图SND无法支持的大规模场景，同时支持被动测量和闭环多样性控制。

Abstract: System Neural Diversity (SND) measures behavioral heterogeneity in multi-agent reinforcement learning by averaging pairwise distances over all $\binom{n}{2}$ agent pairs, making each call quadratic in team size. We introduce Graph-SND, which replaces this complete-graph average with a weighted average over the edges of an arbitrary graph $G$. Three regimes follow: $G=K_n$ recovers SND exactly; a fixed sparse $G$ defines a localized diversity measure at $O(|E|)$ cost; and random edge samples yield an unbiased Horvitz-Thompson estimator and a normalized sample mean with $O(1/\sqrt{m})$ concentration in the sampled edge count $m$. For fixed sparse graphs we prove forwarding-index distortion bounds for expanders and a spectral refinement under low-rank distance structure; for random $d$-regular graphs we prove an unconditional probabilistic $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(D_{\max}/\sqrt{n})$ bound. On VMAS we verify recovery, unbiasedness, concentration, and wall-clock scaling, with a PettingZoo TVD panel checking non-Gaussian transfer. In a 500-iteration $n=100$ PPO run, Bernoulli-$0.1$ Graph-SND tracks full SND while reducing per-call metric time by about $10\times$, and frozen-policy GPU timing up to $n=500$ follows the predicted $\binom{n}{2}/|E|$ speedup. Random $d$-regular expanders empirically achieve $\mathrm{SND}_{G}^{\mathrm{u}}/\mathrm{SND} \in [0.9987, 1.0013]$ at $Θ(n \log n)$ edges. In DiCo diversity control at $n=50$, Bernoulli-$0.1$ Graph-SND preserves set-point tracking with paired reward differences indistinguishable from zero across nine matched cells while cutting per-call metric cost by ${\sim}9.5\times$. Together, these results show that the SND aggregation bottleneck can be removed without changing the metric's semantics, yielding a drop-in sparse alternative that scales beyond complete-graph SND and supports both passive measurement and closed-loop diversity control.

</details>


### [185] [CuBridge: An LLM-Based Framework for Understanding and Reconstructing High-Performance Attention Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05023)
*Xing Ma,Yangjie Zhou,Wu Sun,Zihan Liu,Jingwen Leng,Yun Lin,Shixuan Sun,Minyi Guo,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CuBridge是一个基于大语言模型的框架，通过"提升-转换-降低"流程，将专家编写的CUDA注意力核转换为用户指定的PyTorch版本，生成的核函数在正确性和性能上均优于现有框架、编译器和基于LLM的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的注意力机制CUDA实现在性能和灵活性之间存在权衡：专家手写核效率高但难以适配，通用框架和编译器灵活但性能差，而LLM生成核的正确性和性能不理想。

Method: 提出CuBridge框架，采用三步流程：1) 提升：将专家编写的CUDA核提升为可执行的中间表示(IR)，显式化执行编排；2) 转换：根据用户PyTorch规格生成并验证目标IR程序；3) 降低：通过参考引导将IR降级为优化的CUDA代码。

Result: 在多种注意力变体和GPU平台上，CuBridge一致生成正确的核函数，显著优于通用框架、编译器方法和先前的LLM方法。

Conclusion: CuBridge通过结构化流程有效弥合了专家手写核的高性能和LLM生成灵活性之间的鸿沟，为注意力机制的自动化迁移提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Efficient CUDA implementations of attention mechanisms are critical to modern deep learning systems, yet supporting diverse and evolving attention variants remains challenging. Existing frameworks and compilers trade performance for flexibility, while expert-written kernels achieve high efficiency but are difficult to adapt. Recent work explores large language models (LLMs) for GPU kernel generation, but prior studies report unstable correctness and significant performance gaps for complex operators such as attention.
  We present CuBridge, an LLM-based framework that adapts expert-written attention kernels through a structured lift-transfer-lower workflow. CuBridge starts from expert-written CUDA attention kernels and lifts them into an executable intermediate representation that makes execution orchestration explicit while abstracting low-level CUDA syntax. Given a user-provided PyTorch specification, CuBridge generates and verifies a target IR program, then reconstructs optimized CUDA code via reference-guided lowering. Across diverse attention variants and GPU platforms, CuBridge consistently produces correct kernels and substantially outperforms general frameworks, compiler-based approaches, and prior LLM-based methods.

</details>


### [186] [Full-chip CMP modelling based on Fully Convolutional Network leveraging White Light Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05062)
*Jules Exbrayat,Renan Bouis,Elie Sezestre,Viorel Balan,Arnaud Cornelis,Damien Hebras,Catherine Euvrard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结合白光干涉仪（WLI）和原子力显微镜（AFM）两种表面分析技术，训练基于CNN的深度学习模型，实现纳米级精度的全芯片CMP后纳米形貌预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CMP模型依赖密度台阶高度（DSH）建模，校准耗时且需要大量硬件资源进行细粒度预测，无法满足快速上市的需求。

Method: 结合WLI和AFM两种表面分析技术的优势，采用基于CNN的深度学习模型，通过两步流水线分别用两种技术的数据训练模型。

Result: 实现全芯片后CMP纳米形貌的纳米级精度预测。

Conclusion: 该方法能够加速IC制造中布局可制造性验证，降低成本和缩短设计周期。

Abstract: As time-to-market is crucial in the Integrated Circuit (IC) industry, speeding up layout manufacturability verifi-cation is essential. Chemical-Mechanical Polishing (CMP) plays a vital role in IC fabrication but is significantly influenced by Layout-Dependent Effects (LDE). An accurate and efficient CMP model enables design teams to correct surface unevenness before fabrication, reducing costs and accelerating the design phase. However, existing models often rely on Density Step Height (DSH) modeling, which is time-consuming for calibration and requires substantial hardware resources for fine-grained predictions. In this paper, we propose combining the advantages of two surface analysis techniques, White Light Interfer-ometry (WLI) and Atomic Force Microscopy (AFM), to train a deep learning model. This model aims to predict full-chip post-CMP nanotopography with nanometer-scale accuracy. Our deep learning model is based on a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) and follows a two-step pipeline. The model is trained on each technique separately, resulting in a detailed full-chip CMP model.

</details>


### [187] [Provable imitation learning for control of instability in partially-observed Vlasov--Poisson equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05081)
*Xiaofan Xia,Qin Li,Wenlong Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用模仿学习方法，将基于全相空间状态的理想控制策略蒸馏为仅依赖宏观观测数据的控制器，以稳定Vlasov-Poisson等离子体动力学系统，并证明了学习策略的稳定性保证及误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核聚变中Vlasov-Poisson等离子体动力学的稳定是一个关键控制问题，但实践中理想的控制器需要完整的相空间状态信息，而实验仅能获得稀疏的宏观诊断数据。这一现实差距促使作者研究如何从宏观观测中学习有效的稳定控制策略。

Method: 采用模仿学习（imitation learning）方法，将基于全状态信息的专家策略蒸馏为仅依赖宏观观测的控制器；通过行为克隆损失刻画误差下界，并引入熵的概念量化初始分布的复杂度来表征最小损失。

Result: 证明了学习策略的稳定性保证，误差界取决于观测约束下的最小行为克隆损失；通过熵理论刻画了这一最小损失；数值实验表明学习策略能够在比非自适应基线控制器更长的时间范围内稳定系统。

Conclusion: 从理论上证明了从宏观观测学习稳定化反馈策略的可行性，展示了学习方法对低复杂度结构的自适应性，为核聚变等实际等离子体控制问题提供了理论支撑和方法指导。

Abstract: We consider the stabilization of Vlasov--Poisson plasma dynamics, a central control problem in nuclear fusion. Our focus is the gap between what an ideal controller would use and what experiments can actually observe: while optimal policy may rely on the full phase-space state, practical feedback is typically limited to sparse macroscopic diagnostics. We therefore study imitation learning methods that distill a fully observed expert policy into controllers operating only on macroscopic measurements. We show the stability guarantees of the learned policy, where the error floor depends on the minimal behavior cloning loss achievable under the observation constraints. We further characterize this minimal loss in terms of a notion of entropy that quantifies the complexity of the initial distribution. Our results demonstrates the theoretical feasibility of learning stabilizing feedback policies for kinetic plasma dynamics from macroscopic observations, and exhibits the adaptivity of the learning approach to low-complexity structures. Through extensive numerical experiments, we validate our theory and show that the learned policies can stabilize the system using only macroscopic observations, within a significantly longer time horizon than non-adaptive baseline controllers.

</details>


### [188] [Order Matters: Improving Domain Adaptation by Reordering Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05084)
*Andrea Napoli,Paul White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORDERED通过优化数据采样顺序来降低域差异估计的方差，从而提升无监督域自适应的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督域自适应中，域差异估计在随机设置下存在高方差问题，这削弱了理论优势。

Method: 提出ORDERED，一种无偏随机方差缩减技术，通过优化训练数据的采样顺序来降低两种特定域差异损失（相关对齐和最大均值差异）的估计误差。

Result: 模拟实验显示方差低于相关方法；在两个域偏移图像分类基准上，目标域准确率得到提升。

Conclusion: 优化数据采样顺序可有效降低域差异估计方差，提升UDA模型在目标域上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Domain shift remains a key challenge in deploying machine learning models to the real world. Unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) aims to address this by minimising domain discrepancy during training, but the discrepancy estimates suffer from high variance in stochastic settings, which can stifle the theoretical benefits of the method. This paper proposes Optimal Reordering of Data for Error-Reduced Estimation of Discrepancy (ORDERED), a novel unbiased stochastic variance reduction technique which reduces the discrepancy estimation error by optimising the order in which the training data are sampled. We consider two specific domain discrepancy losses (correlation alignment and the maximum mean discrepancy), formulate their stochastic estimation error as a function of the data sampling order, and propose a practical optimisation algorithm. Our simulations demonstrate reduced variance compared to related methods, and experiments on two domain shift image classification benchmarks show improved target domain accuracy.

</details>


### [189] [Gated Multimodal Learning for Interpretable Property Energy Performance Prediction and Retrofit Scenario Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05088)
*Yunfei Bai,Aaron Tesfa Tsion,Raul Rosales,Barbara Shollock,Wei He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种门控多模态模型，通过整合EPC表格变量、评估师文本和GIS空间特征，预测住宅建筑能效等级，在伦敦 Westminster 案例中实现 MAE 4.39，并应用于 retrofit 改造情景分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅建筑脱碳是实现可持续城市的关键，但现有EPC依赖现场检查，难以进行城市尺度的及时评估，亟需可扩展的预测方法。

Method: 构建门控多模态模型，融合EPC表格数据、评估师自由文本、GIS空间特征；采用样本级门控学习属性特定模态权重；辅助波段分类头稳定训练。

Result: 模型在SAP和EI评分预测上MAE分别为4.03和4.76，R2为0.757和0.748；多模态融合优于单/双模态基线；门控权重显示强依赖评估师文本；SHAP突出主燃料、建筑形式和年代。

Conclusion: 该框架能为改造筛查、干预优先级排序和净零住房转型提供可扩展的物业级证据支持。

Abstract: Achieving resilient and sustainable cities requires scalable approaches to decarbonising residential buildings, which account for about 20% of UK greenhouse gas emissions and 25% of energy-related emissions in the European Union.
